# Palindrome sentence generator: results ledger

Generated from **2,552 committed JSON artifacts** on 2026-09-22 at `b9ffbeb5`.

This ledger is evidence-first: it includes every committed method artifact, distinguishes rendered candidate length from independently audited exact length, and keeps exactness separate from prose quality. Paths point only to committed files so the report remains reproducible after checkout.

## Current frontier

The promoted readability frontier is **666 letters** in [`central-mini-scene-comparison-leon-noel-666`](../runs/incumbent-666-central-mini-scene-comparison-20260922.json). It is independently exact (`two_pointer_exact=True`, `sha_equal=True`) and has SHA-256 `3951b9449ed3ab28f55d9798e344dfaf3123035f5f07c9dffdf0047bed0e1d79`. The working incumbent remains the separately recorded 568-letter child; promotion improves the comparison seam while carrying the documented construction debt.

An exact **588-letter 568-lineage rewrite** is retained for audit ([run artifact](../runs/incumbent-568-linked-event-pair-growth-20260923.json), SHA-256 `957f2db34fde364866217a45c4bcc87c412a510b264490cc1a701c60c07651c0`), but is **not admitted**: all six inserted tokens pair one-by-one with their reversed right-side tokens, and `sees` is self-palindromic. A later 604-letter shell edit replayed an already-used seam and is also not counted. Neither is a readability result.

```text
Leon won. Wolf spots Nora. Nora sees Nadia. Nadia sees Ira. Sara saw God. Ari saw Dog. Mara stops rats. Nora spots a ram. Mara sees rats. Nora stops rats. Nora sees Aram. Mara sees Nadia. Nadia saw Noel live. Mara stops Nadia. Nora sees Aram. Sara saw Noel live. Now, Noel, did I live? Nora saw Noel live. Noel, I sit. Pat notes. Mara saw God. Sara, did I live? Nora, I saw desserts. Leon stops Noel. Noel spots Nadia. Nadia stops Aidan. Ari sees God. Dog sees Ira. Nadia spots Aidan. Aidan stops Leon. Leon spots Noel. Stressed was I, Aron. Evil I did, Aras. Dog was Aram. Seton, tap. 'Tis I, Leon. “Evil Leon” was Aron. Evil I did, Leon won. Evil Leon was Aras. Mara sees Aron. Aidan spots Aram. Evil Leon was Aidan. Aidan sees Aram. Mara sees Aron; star spots Aron. Star sees Aram. Mara stops Aron. Star spots Aram. God was Ira. Dog was Aras. Ari sees Aidan. Aidan sees Aron. Aron stops flow now, Noel.
```

## Luna 6 continuation addendum — 2026-09-23

The 568-lineage experiments load the pinned parent in `runs/incumbent-560-outer-causal-scene-20261002.json` (normalized SHA-256 `6647fe46becb64b0841785f0bd9865070254888b449be228d22cfbedeb1e0380`). The separately labeled 560-frontier experiment loads `runs/incumbent-550-central-event-bridge-20261002.json` (normalized SHA-256 `b5f98bfb0b44b31d8cbf78727672a74b588980e1fc8f1ff522a2c4ad1d800ccc`), and the 558-frontier tests load `runs/incumbent-550-typed-center-product-20261002.json` row `typed-center-25` (normalized SHA-256 `29470b5ab408c402e8796530123357fea6a74aa4bdf14f7f1b2a601dbecc94fa`). The 568 parent remains the working incumbent; none of these diagnostics is reader-validated.

Two other lanes were retired before candidate emission: a three-owner map on `[20,48)/[520,548)` collides with the incumbent's already-used causal event map, and the proposed 70-letter scene pair on `[135,232)/cut 433` had no admissible lexicalization after rejecting a whole-token-reversal lead. These are tracked as preflight/obstruction evidence, not as candidate results. The 560-frontier scene is an inexact diagnostic, not an admitted palindrome. Both 558 seam attempts now have concrete mismatch records. The 556 preflight found no fresh outer partial-word map: its `delivers` and `maps` equations are subowners of the existing outer scene, while the next inner seam is the prohibited `i|ts` lineage. The 568 remains the working incumbent.
